# inputRichMessage

**Layer** : 227

```tl
inputRichMessage#e4c449fc flags:# rtl:flags.0?true noautolink:flags.1?true blocks:Vector<PageBlock> photos:flags.2?Vector<InputPhoto> documents:flags.3?Vector<InputDocument> users:flags.4?Vector<InputUser> = InputRichMessage;
```

---

## Parameters

| Name | Type | Description |
| :---: | :---: | :--- |
| <del style = 'opacity : 0.4'>flags</del> | [`#`](type/#) | NOTHING |
| **rtl** | [`flags.0?true`](type/true) | NOTHING |
| **noautolink** | [`flags.1?true`](type/true) | NOTHING |
| <mark>blocks</mark> | [`Vector<PageBlock>`](type/PageBlock) | NOTHING |
| **photos** | [`flags.2?Vector<InputPhoto>`](type/InputPhoto) | NOTHING |
| **documents** | [`flags.3?Vector<InputDocument>`](type/InputDocument) | NOTHING |
| **users** | [`flags.4?Vector<InputUser>`](type/InputUser) | NOTHING |

---

## Type

[InputRichMessage](type/InputRichMessage)
